# FINDINGS-2 (untriaged): task-369 COMPLY.5, second handbook compliance pass on git diff 9f04d9074..3a36e8b26 (Specs 126, 127, 128 and the full Spec 135 rows)

Lane BL-COMPLY3, worktree head 5357e5bc8. Nothing here is adjudicated or fixed. A finding is a BUG or DEFER line returned by the DeepSeek compliance lens (.claude/review-templates/lens-compliance.md), copied as returned; ids continue the first pass (task-365, last id F-491) and begin at F-492. The only line added by this lane per finding is "Opened:" (file:line opened with git show at 3a36e8b26: the quoted code is there or is not there; a mechanical text check, so the grounder opens it). Every finding goes to the separate grounder (lane BL-GROUND, task-368). MET and REJECT lines are not findings; they are in the pass2-*.txt files.

## Method (same as the first pass, FINDINGS.md "Method and tool limits found"; deviations stated)

- One lens run per (pack part, diff part): pack in `--context` (sent once), diff part as the file (sent twice by the template), command form `node -r dotenv/config scripts/deepseek-review.js review <diff part> --template .claude/review-templates/lens-compliance.md --context <pack part>` with the env-file path set by the allowed variable form. Every `$` in the pack and in the diff was doubled and the three placeholder tokens neutralised (zero-width space between the braces) in the copies sent (lane dir `esc/`); the packs and diffs committed are the unescaped originals. The lens returns at most 8 findings per run, so a run that returned 8 may have had more.
- The packs are the four sources WHOLE and VERBATIM (generated by `build-packs.mjs`; checked line by line by `verify-packs.mjs`, output in the table below). Split points are the source's own headings (## sections, then ### where a ## section was over the bound). One deviation from "split only by the source's own headings": in Spec 135 the whole register is ONE heading section (`## Register`, 72720 characters, 48 rows of a table) which cannot fit the bound, so it was split at table row boundaries; parts fe-135-p3 and p4 repeat the table header (2 lines) after the range marker, labelled as repeated and not part of the range (the verifier skips it). Nothing was omitted: none of the four sources marks any section as history-only (grep -i for "history" and "historical" in the four files found only line mentions inside rule text, 126:193, 126:508, 126:667, 126:894, 126:898, 127: none, 128:232, 128:255, 135:42; none is a section marked as history), so no heading was left out.
- Rule-bearing lines are counted by pattern (stated in each pack header): lines matching `must|MUST|never|NEVER|shall|SHALL|forbidden|required|RULED|Rule N|B1-B6`, or a table row that opens a register row (`| FE-nn`, `| **R-nn`, `| **ASK-nn`, `| **Pn`). It is a count of lines that look rule-bearing, not a count of rules; the sources hold no per-rule enforcement field.
- Diff parts: the lens saw only the production parts these specs bind; `git diff --name-only 9f04d9074 3a36e8b26` gave 510 files, of which the ones under scripts/surfaces/, src/components/surfaces/, src/lib/surfaces/, mobile/src/components/surfaces/, mobile/app/, mobile/src/, src/app/api/parcels/lookup/, src/app/api/parcels/entitlement/ and src/lib/entitlements/ were taken. Excluded (not sent): mobile/src/generated/surfaces/mobile_parcel_disambiguation_map.json; mobile/src/generated/surfaces/mobile_parcel_search.json (generated slices); no test, golden or fixture file lies under those paths. Also not sent, and not in this pass: the rest of the 510 files (src/lib/parcels, src/lib/report-projection, report page and component files, scripts/analysis, tests and the 160 golden/fixture files), which the brief did not name.
